[EVENT: IRQ45602]
Date: 2023-04-03
Country: Iraq | Location: Sulaymaniyah
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Rudaw [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 3 April 2023, families and relatives of autistic children protested in Sulaymaniyah city. The protesters demanded better services and facilities for autistic people. The protest was organized on the World Autism Awareness Day. Families of autistic people received 150,00 Iraqi Dinars (about 115 USD a month) but the protesters stated their real expenses are 400,000 Iraqi dinars (about 300 USD) a month.

---

The protesters denounced an annual tax hike to 2,500,000 Iraqi Dinars (about 1,900 USD) stating the tax change is not in line with their profit margins. The protesters threatened to go on strike if the tax rate is not reversed to the previous level.

[EVENT: IRQ45972]
Date: 2023-05-11
Country: Iraq | Location: Qaryat Shanaf
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Iraq (2022-2026) [State forces]
Actor2: Islamic State in Iraq and the Levant (ISIL) [Rebel group]
Fatalities: 5
Sources: Al Naba; National Iraqi News Agency; Patriotic Union of Kurdistan; Shafaaq News [Subnational-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 11 May 2023, 4 IS militants dressed in military uniforms killed a farmer and stole his vehicle in the Tal Hamid and Qaryat Shanaf area in Ninewa. After which, the IS militants clashed with the 30th Brigade (Shabak Militia) of the Popular Mobilization Forces and Iraqi army in the area. The Popular Mobilization Forces sustained 1 fatality and 1 injured while the army sustained 2 injured. The Popular Mobilization Forces, Army, and unspecified tribal militants then counter-attacked the IS militants killing 3 and detaining 1. IS claimed that the PMF sustained 3 fatalities. 5 fatalities coded.

On 20 May 2023, Turkish army tanks, artillery, and unspecified heavy weapons shelled PKK positions in Cemco (Cheme Ci) area in Al Amadiya district of Duhok. Casualties unknown.

[EVENT: IRQ46365]
Date: 2023-05-20
Country: Iraq | Location: Sinjar
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: ANF News; ROJ News [Regional-International]

DESCRIPTION:
On 20 May 2023, Yazidi and women groups staged a demonstration in Sinjar town of Ninewa to express solidarity with the residents of Makhmur Camp in Erbil protesting against the Iraqi army's ongoing operation to establish a perimeter fence and a single entrance to secure the camp.
